```text
PRODUCT BLUEPRINT: APPROVED — LOCKED (UNCHANGED)
EIS: APPROVED — LOCKED (UNCHANGED)
IMPLEMENTATION PACKAGE: DRAFT — STAGE 13 REVIEW REQUIRED
IMPLEMENTATION AUTHORIZATION: WITHHELD — IMPLEMENTATION NOT AUTHORIZED
SCHEMA / SQL / MIGRATIONS: NOT AUTHORIZED
SCHEDULER / AI PROVIDER / EXTERNAL CHANNEL ACTIVATION: NOT AUTHORIZED
PRODUCTION MUTATION / DELIVERY: NOT AUTHORIZED
```

## 2. Purpose and Authority

This contract turns the locked SB-P-1.15 EIS into implementation obligations. Each obligation `EC15-<suffix>` realizes exactly one locked EIS requirement `EIS15-<suffix>`, in the EIS section named, with the requirement's row mapping from EIS Section 29.2 unchanged. It adds no Product Truth, changes no FCTM row, Blueprint scope, Founder decision or mission ownership, and resolves no Founder-reserved question.

It is a future engineering specification. **It authorizes no build.** Building requires Stage 13 package approval **and** a separate Implementation Authorization, which under the Phase 1 operating pattern remains **WITHHELD — IMPLEMENTATION NOT AUTHORIZED**.

## 5. Authorized Scope and Exclusions

### 5.1 In scope

The 694 `IN SCOPE` rows of the canonical FCTM, each mapped in Section 12, realized through the 147 obligations of Section 7.

### 5.2 Not authorized by this package

Code, schema, SQL, migrations or their execution; scheduler runtime selection or activation; AI provider selection, configuration or activation; any external notification channel; secrets or environment changes; Supabase or production mutation; deployment, release or delivery; execution of any test in the Verification Checklist; any Stage 14 or Stage 15 work.

### 5.3 Still committed, not in this mission

| FCTM disposition | Rows | Treatment |
|---|---|---|
| `ASSIGNED TO LATER MISSION` | 25 | Built by the owning later mission, never here |
| `ASSIGNED TO LATER MISSION — HELD (U-3)` | 1 | `20-§23-10`: SB-P-1.15 enforces the approved schedule and owns `EIS15-VER-LEG`; SB-P-1.19 owns onboarding acceptance (MC-80); outside the 694 |
| `DELEGATED` | 40 | Owned by the delegate mission; consumed, not rebuilt |
| `NOT APPLICABLE` | 23 | No build |
| `OUT OF BUILD SCOPE` | 20 | Must not appear (Build Plan §16 rejections, including custom client-specific POS modification inside core) |
| `UNRESOLVED FOUNDER DECISION` | 1 | Not built until decided |

## 6. Obligation Types

| Type | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `BUILD — DEPENDENCY GATED` | Implement exactly as the locked EIS specifies, under a future Implementation Authorization, only after the row's upstream holds (`D12`, `D13`, `D14`, `DTX`, later missions) are satisfied by implemented and accepted upstream work. |
| `BUILD — DC-06 PROPOSED` | Implement the deny-by-default enforcement mechanism; the capability catalogue, manifest content and required-scope sets stay **PROPOSED** and gain no merchant-facing grant, label, default or delegation right without Founder T6/T3. |
| `BUILD — PROVIDER GATED` | Implement behind the SB-P-1.13 gateway; no merchant data reaches a model until provider evidence is accepted and activation separately authorized. |
| `BUILD — RUNTIME GATED` | Implement against the runtime-neutral scheduler contract; runtime selection and activation need separate authorization. |
| `MIGRATION-DESIGN` | Fixes objects, access contract, ordering, rollback, forward-fix and rehearsal. Writing or executing a migration needs an explicit migration mission under `docs/migration/README.md`. |
| `VERIFICATION` | Defines verification architecture and acceptance evidence. Adds no product behaviour. |
| `GOVERNANCE` | Applies to every builder workstream; constrains how behaviour is built and adds none of its own. |
| `GATE` | Not builder work. Mission Control enforces it through dependency evidence before dependent obligations are built or demonstrated. |

## 7. Engineering Obligations

Verification references name the Verification Checklist's negative-path checks (`NP-*`, `SR6-*`) and runtime scenarios (`RS-*`); every row also has its row-keyed item in Verification Checklist Section 14.4. Row lists of 25 or fewer are shown; larger lists are exactly the rows citing the obligation in Section 12. Phases order work and authorize none of it.

## 8. Cross-Cutting Engineering Rules

These rules apply to every builder workstream and restate locked EIS constraints; they add no behaviour.

1. **One authorization kernel.** Every read and command calls the SB-P-1.12 kernel (`authz.authorize`, `EIS-KER-01`) from the verified principal and explicit active business. No feature-local permission logic, no AI- or trigger-created permission (`EC15-AUTHZ-01`, `EC15-PRIV-04`).
2. **One governed transaction write boundary.** No SB-P-1.15 component writes the Ledger. Every financial effect goes through the single governed transactions-family command (`DTX`/PQ-2; SB-P-1.13 `EIS13-CMD-02`, SB-P-1.14 `EIS14-CMD-01`/`-02`) with an effect key. Ask CFO and Daily Intelligence cannot reach it (`EC15-FIN-05`, `EC15-RUL-05`, `EC15-CFO-02`).
3. **Commands are the only write boundary** for SB-P-1.15 tables; no direct client `INSERT`/`UPDATE`/`DELETE` (`EIS-ISO-02`).
4. **Current-principal and business scope.** Business is server-derived and persisted on every work item; composite `(business_id, id)` references; a worker never switches business (`EC15-SEC-03`, `EC15-PRIV-03`).
5. **DC-06 required-scope AND semantics.** Every intelligence object has one manifest row with its complete `required_scopes`; access requires the caller's current scopes to contain **every** listed scope; `authz.require_scopes` is the normative primitive; unmapped objects are denied, including to the Owner; no OR semantics; Managers have no intelligence access by role alone. The catalogue stays **DC-06 — PROPOSED** (`EC15-AUTHZ-04`, `-08` to `-11`).
6. **Financial fact provenance.** Facts carry `derives_from`, `required_scopes`, source watermark, calculation version and manifest version; scope is checked before the first read; no Owner-wide result is redacted afterwards; derived metrics need every input scope; provenance closure, filter-class and uniform-denial rules bound inference (`EC15-FIN-01`, `-03`, `-06`).
7. **Correction-aware history.** Corrected values are used with originals preserved; past briefings are immutable; Ledger dates are never reinterpreted (`EC15-FIN-02`, `EC15-TZ-07`).
8. **Owner-confirmed timezone.** Append-only versions and events; Owner-only, non-delegable confirm and change; activation at the next business-day boundary in the new zone; no default, backfill or fabricated occurrence (`EC15-TZ-01` to `-10`).
9. **Scheduler identity.** Logical work, attempt, lease, fence and effect are separate; stale fences commit nothing; unknown outcomes resolve by effect-key lookup; only 07:00, 10:30 and 22:00 business-local slots exist (`EC15-SCH-01` to `-11`).
10. **Native first.** No external channel before SB-P-1.20; requests are never delivery; FPDR-02 prospective-only correction notifications; FPDR-03 latest-valid briefing provenance (`EC15-NTF-*`, `EC15-DI-03`, `-04`).
11. **Zero-write Ask CFO and prompt-injection containment** (`EC15-CFO-02`, `-03`, `EC15-AI-02`).
12. **Standard POS bridge only.** No client-specific modification or fork inside the core (`22-§17-1`, `22-§17-9`, `22-§28-10`, `BP-§16-7`).
13. **Truthful status.** Nothing is described as deployed, passed or delivered without evidence (`EC15-ARC-03`, `EC15-UX-03`).

### 8.1 Database object ownership, RLS, grants and privileged functions

Planned only; created by migration groups M1–M8 under a separately authorized migration mission.

| Object group | Migration group | Owner role | Access contract | Workstream |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `business_timezone_versions`, `business_timezone_events`, `business_timezone_heads`, `business_timezone_suggestions` | M1 | Migration owner | RLS by business; writes only via `tz.confirm`, `tz.change`, `tz.activate_due`; head row changed only under row lock | WS-C |
| `sched_slot_registry`, `sched_work`, `sched_attempts`, `sched_effects`, `sched_quarantine` | M2 | Migration owner | Registry `CHECK` on slot codes; scheduler worker role writes only `sched_*`; fence checked on every write | WS-C |
| `reminders`, `reminder_rule_versions`, `reminder_occurrences`, `reminder_events` | M3 | Migration owner | RLS by business and owner scope; commands only | WS-A |
| `standing_rules`, `standing_rule_versions`, `standing_rule_runs` | M4 | Migration owner | RLS by business; grantor current authority at run and commit | WS-B |
| `notification_requests`, `notification_states`, `notification_activation` | M5 | Migration owner | RLS by business and recipient; native states only; no channel states | WS-D |
| `di_slots`, `di_briefing_revisions`, `di_briefing_sections`, `di_briefing_narratives` | M6 | Migration owner | Revisions metadata only; sections and narratives readable only when `authz.has_all_scopes` holds | WS-E |
| `intel` schema views and functions; `cfo_reader` role | M7 | Migration owner | `security_barrier`, `security_invoker` views with explicit columns; invoker functions with `authz.require_scopes` before the first read; no `PUBLIC`/`anon` `EXECUTE`; `cfo_reader` has no base-table or command grants | WS-E, WS-F |
| Capability registrations, `intel_access_manifest`, `intel_fact_classes` | M8 | Migration owner | Versioned data; completeness test against the catalog | WS-G |
| `SECURITY DEFINER` command wrappers | M1–M8 | Migration owner | Call `authz.authorize` and, for intelligence objects, `authz.require_scopes` inside the body; pinned `search_path`; declared `EXECUTE` matrix; never accept caller-supplied decisions | All |

### 8.2 Migration, rollback, restore, recovery and reconciliation

Order: D12 → D13 → D14/`DTX` → M1 to M8. Each group is birth-safe and rehearsed on the isolated test project. Before production data exists, a failed group is rolled back; after data exists, failures are fixed forward. After a backup or PITR restore, open work is re-fenced, `UNKNOWN` effects are reconciled by effect-key lookup before any retry, notification requests are re-derived by dedupe key, and legacy artifacts are staged in an isolated, ungranted schema and reconciled into quarantine (`EC15-MIG-01` to `-07`, `EC15-VER-LEG`). Production migration is NOT AUTHORIZED.

## 10. Founder Decision Boundaries

| Decision | Boundary carried |
|---|---|
| FPDR-01 | Ask CFO read-only and zero-write; FDB-1 overlay kept, (d2) via the MC-75 D1 corrective mission; FDB-2 native-only; FDB-3 qualified entry-level credit answers, authoritative balances in SB-P-1.16 |
| FPDR-02 | Correction notifications prospective-only from an audited activation boundary; no historical replay; no bulk status rewrite |
| FPDR-03 | Latest valid native briefing with scheduled slot, generation time and source as-of; no duplicate; no invented staleness cutoff; no late outbound |
| MC-74, MC-75 D2/D3 | Owner-only confirmation; non-authoritative suggestions; saved reminders UNSCHEDULED; changes at the next business-day boundary in the new zone |
| MC-75 D1 | Corrective mission route only; `H-CORR` unactivated |
| MC-80 (U-3) | `20-§23-10` split: SB-P-1.15 schedule enforcement and regression; SB-P-1.19 onboarding acceptance |
| DC-06 | **PROPOSED**; no merchant-facing grant, label, default bundle or delegation right without Founder T6/T3 |

## 14. Phase 1 Boundary and Suspension Path

This package is an engineering specification, not authorization to execute. After Stage 13, even an `APPROVED — LOCKED` package leaves **Implementation Authorization: WITHHELD — IMPLEMENTATION NOT AUTHORIZED** unless a later governed Founder decision changes the Phase 1 operating direction. No Stage 14 or Stage 15 work begins because the package is complete.

The eventual documentary suspension path, to be created only under separate authority after Stage 13 and the withheld authorization, is: Documentation & Execution-Readiness Completion Checkpoint; Partial Completion and Suspension Report; Residual Work & Exact Resume Register; Communication Suspension and Archive Deferment Record; Partial OLE Handoff, with the statuses **COMMUNICATION SUSPENDED — FORMAL ARCHIVE DEFERRED** and **FORMAL OLE CLOSURE ENVELOPE NOT CREATED — MISSION NOT FORMALLY CLOSED**. These records are not Stage 12 outputs.
